yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷi nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmi kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunili niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷi ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkum tolliyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēviyumilli. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉi aṭiyalām.
- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nāki - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulami mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam uṭiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟinta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭimiyē eṉṉi ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toki curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷi varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭi	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷi
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟi maṟintatē
aṭikaḷār vāḻntatu mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭē yāyiṉum, avarāl tamiḻnāṭu aṭinta payaṉ aḷapparitu eṉak kūṟutal mikiyākātu. aṭikaḷār varaṉmuṟi vaḻātu mummoḻip pulami peṟṟa mutuperum pulavar; civa cittāntattiṉ tiṭpanuṭpaṅkaḷi naṉku payiṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarum meccum vakiyil ‘Mystic Mina’ eṉṉum āṅkila veḷiyīṭṭiṉ mūlam eṅkum paravacceytavar. aṭikaḷār pulamiyiṟ paḻamiyum putumiyum paṅku peṟṟōṅkiṉa. aṭikaḷār payiṉṟa nūlkaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟaṉa eṉpati maṟintaṉa oḻiya iruntaṉavāyoḷirum nūlkaḷiyuṭiya avarkaḷatu nūṟkaruvūlamē iṉitu viḷakkum.
aṭikaḷār nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulap celvarāy - nāvīṟuṭiyarāy - eḻuttāṟṟalil evarum viyakkattakka eḻuttāḷarāy ilaṅkiyum, avarkaḷ avvāṟṟalkaḷi aṭimi vāḻvilīṭupaṭṭu vipaḻalukkiṟitta vīṇirākkāmal, urimi vīṟuṭaṉ taṉimiyiluḻittup poruḷ pukaḻum īṭṭi, viyattu vāḻvāṅku vāḻnta vaki peritum pārāṭṭattakkatu. avvakiyil aṭikaḷār māperuṅ kalivīrar eṉap pōṟṟutaṟkuriyār.
uṟaṅkuvatu pōlum cākkā ṭuṟaṅki
  viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu 	(kuṟaḷ 339)
eṉṉum uṇmiyi uḷaṅkoṇṭu, kaḻintataṟ kiraṅkātu, nammip pirinta nallavar iṉṉuyir avartam vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuḷiṉ maṟimaliyaraṭikaḷiṉ maṉṉi vaṟṟā iṉpam uṟṟu viḷaṅkaveṉa maṉaṅkoḷalaṉṟi maṟṟeṉceya vallōm!
- vittuvāṉ mē.vī. vēṇukōpāla piḷḷi.
(pak. 47)

maṟimaliya māṇpu
nuḻivuri
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
munti aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum takavārnta perumi peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yurikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟi moḻikaḷukkum tanti moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum perumi peṟṟa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭi maliyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpati nili nāṭṭiya niṟimali, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuttāl orupāṭi eṉṟu aṟiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalimali pēḻiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟimi pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpati,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunili tirital ceyyuṭ killi 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇi ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumiyum kuṟumiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷi ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟimali aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takiniṉṟa taṇṭamiḻi - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟimali yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivi
 pintamiḻ kākkum paṭi
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭiyum kalit tiṟap paṭippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟi āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟi āṟṟali 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti
paṉimali mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amintavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimali - imayam; nīla āṟu - nil; amitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷi, nūlaka iyakkat tanti araṅka nātarum, innāḷ civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakimippaṭutti vittami perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avi,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭuri 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nāki aṭikaḷi - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammiyār! ivviṇiyar koṭiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉi ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷatti vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvari payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum niliyil, arumit tantiyār iyaṟki eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyit toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmi tamiḻukku illi. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppilli eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟiyiyum, nākiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟiyiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟiyāl vāṭum aṉṉikkuc cumiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉi māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpatiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyi niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, toki nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi, iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvi mukappil, ematu patiṉintām āṇṭil muṟippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyatiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uriyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nāki intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc civa cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉi maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉipeyaril maṟuppuri eḻutiṉār. ataṉikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉi virivilē ceṉṉikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉi vāḻvu amiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉi māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumi kaṇṭu, pērāciriyarik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uriyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattiyum mūtaṟiviyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanili ovvāmiyāl nākikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉi: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaric ceṉṉikku aḻittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpiki eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉi kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟit talivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kaliñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkali māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappāli (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci uri eḻutiṉār.
avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟi uri, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭatti viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikki kuṟiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyi muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. viyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉi kacaṭukaḷi nīkkiyatu? viyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟic ceyta arumi eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal kāriki āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārit taḻuvutal uṇmi tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapuratti vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkikaḷiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷi oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. inniliyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkikaḷiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉiyār ciṉṉammiyār, tuṇiviyār cavuntiram ammi yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvarik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpiki, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. inniliyilēyē 27.08.1911il civat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭi (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. muṉṉēyē avari aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvi
nām mīḷpārvi oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akaviyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvuri vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉi; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉi; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉi - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭami eṉpatu, akattē amintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷi viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa niliyām. av veḷippaṭṭa niliyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravilli! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka niliyam, potunilik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amippu uruvākum niliyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avi iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa niliyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭit tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭit tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭit tūṇṭal valimi mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyi veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takimi ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉi uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmi,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟi yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantip pēṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟoki, arumaṇik kuviyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumit tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 vari mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavi. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaviyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉami, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷi mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. anniliyil,
 ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar uliviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyi vāymi moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amintu tāṅkal, iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟi muṉvittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icik kalikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākaratti veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉi vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉip pakutikaḷiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, urivēntar auvi cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷiyum muḻumi muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - toki - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumi iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇiyiyum makkaḷiyum uṟaviyum aṉpiyum naṇpiyum oruṅkē iṇittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇippiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟimaliya māṇpu
maṟimaliyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumi! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭit tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭi! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakimi! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymi viḷakkamām!
 puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar niliyam,						iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							        irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturi - 6
625006.

malikka vikkum maṟimaliyam!
patippuri
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉi vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmi uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇari aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattit tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭi eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭi niṉivu kūrum vakiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum `maṟimaliyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyi eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭippukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻi 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavi, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavi, peyar terintum pārvikkuk kiṭṭātavi - eṉap palavakiyāyk kiṭittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭippukaḷ! avaṟṟi `maṟimaliyam eṉum talippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavaliyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt tici nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputiyal palar kikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟimali yaṭikaḷārit tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyatti etirppatilum camaṟkirutatti vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyit tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukiyiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kaliñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvi avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta viruti civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malimaliyaṭikaḷ).
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyarik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟimiku āṅkilam maṟivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulami mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
 vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
  maṟimali yaṭikaḷ maṟiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉi maṟimali yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamilli.
ceṉṉiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talimuṟikkum varuṅkālat talimuṟikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉi (cātaṉi)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avi kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpati aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalatti otukki vittu, tamiḻ nalattik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalip piṉṉiṇippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻikkum uṇarvi iḷamiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmi eḻavittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avari eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavi! avaṟṟil orupakutiyi yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vi. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!

Preface 
The first part of the following treatise was read as Presidential Address at the All South India Saiva Con­ference held at Cuddalore, New Town, on the 16th, 17th, 18th, 19th, 20th and 21st 1929. The object of the Con­ference was to revive the study and practice of the oldest principles of the Saiva religion, which, in recent years, have greatly fallen into disuse and disrepute by being mixed up with the Puranic myths and which the modern Self­Respect movement calumniated in the most opprobrious language, without being able to discriminate between the cardinal points of Saivism and the merely adventitious ones. 
In the conflict of thoughts and ethical sentiments that arose between the Tamilians of the south and the Aryans of the north who came and settled all over the Tamil country from the first century A. D., down to the fifteenth and still later, the religion of the land, that is Saivism. underwent a marked change its outer rim, although in its centre it remined as pure as crystal and as impenetrable as a hard diamond. What is bound and true to its core, what is perfect and complete in itself, requires no change, re­quires no improvement. At the very dawn of Tamilian civilisation, of which we catch a glimpse through the Thol­kappiam the oldest Tamil work extant, we find the views about God, soul, and matter and salvation to have become thoroughly sound and complete. As our knowledge of these facts deepens, as our ideas about their relations expand, we find the conclusions reached by our forefathers about their nature and relations, dily receiving corroborations not only from the modern biological, geological, and other  scienitific discoveries, but also from the philosophical view worked out and held by such eminent thinkers as William James, Bergson, Lodge, Ward and others. 
While preserving the intrinsic nature and excellence of their religious system, the teachers and prophets of the Tamil country have not disdined to take from foreign cults. whatever seemed to have influenced the ordinary people, and whatever might be turned to good account in the light of their own religious thought. In this wise came to by added to the outer surface of the body of Saivism, simple as poetic embellishments remember, only a few Puranic­myths of the northeners at first, but, as time went on, rushed in quite a multitude of them which, instead of adding to the naked beauty of the resplendent form, concealed it and even rendered it ugly in the eyes of- an incutious, indolent, or an ill-intentioned looker-on. Only to the penetrating mental vision of a careful thinker, the intrinsic value of the system came to reveal itself. Others touched only its outer crust of myths, some of them believing them to be actual facts, while some others who had an inkling of reasoning. spirit, turned away from them with disgust. However, one who would like to be benefited by the inestimable knowledge treasured up in the Saiva system of thought, should not render himself liable to, be thus scared away by the Aryan myths that sit at its door. Dr. G. U. Pope who did an invaluable Service to: the world by translating into English the sacred Tamil poems called the Thiruva­chakam of S1, Manickavachakar wisely observes: “pura­nic mythology and legend is dragged in, as simple poetic embellishment designed to please the multitude”* 
But the religious and philosophical ideas that are developed here in the following pages have nothing to do with such Puranic myths, for they are taken entirely from the ancient Tamil work the Tholkappiam, the only treatise extant that was composed at a time when the Aryan in­fluence did not touch even the fringe of the Tamilian thought, when the creation of Puranic myths did not take place as yet. The age of this unique Tamil work goes back to 3500 B. C. as is shown in my work on S1, Manicka­vachakar. The Aryans were then spreading over the north mid founding colonies in the midst of the Tamils and Kolarians that existed there long before their advent. 
The Tholkappiam comprises both Tamil grammar and poetics and the latter classifies the ideal activities of the ancient day Tamilians under the three categories, Love Wealh, and Virtue. What to choose from their life for ideal representations in poetry and how to idealize them for which purpose, from the subjects of many a chapter in the third book on poetics. The life of the Tamils thus dwelt on exhibits its gradual emergence from the life of the most primitive kind into that of strikingly civilized one. Still a strong tinge of the primitive characteristics, even in the life of a highly civilized kind, cannot be ex­plined except on the supposition that the Tamils are a people naturally very conservative in their habits and cus­toms and that a large portion of them were then closely adhering to them even while they were adopting the ele­ments of civilisation introduced by the best among them. 
Of the many interresting subjects treated of, Love and War form the two themes that hold as prominent a place in it, as ,they had held in almost all famous literature of the world. For man was born in love, was bred in love lives in love, and passes at last into love. Care has been, therefore, taken by the author to distinguish this love from lust which is intense selfishness, which takes no thought of others and their happiness, and which is wrongly identified with the former by persons who know no more than what they sensuously feel and enjoy. The union of spirits and not the union of mere corporal matter was regarded as the result of love, by 8t. Tholkappiar and by a host of poets, salOts and sages who followed in his footsteps. Love regenerates both mind and body; it springs from the depth of a pure mind, flows out through the body and enters agin into another mind by means of another body and blends the two minds into one. This mortal body serves only as an indispensable instrument for bringing about this intimate union of two pure spirits. When such a superior kind of love springs up from a mind and flows over sweeping before its irresistible and dignified march, all the inferior and harmful kinds of desires and passions, it goes not towards one mind alane, although it runs into one fit­ting centre;: for filling it up with essence, but it ges into each and all and drenches them in its wholesome element in varying degree according to their deserts. Many a~ are the forms and degrees of love, the highest, the greatest and the sublimest manifestation of it occurs only at the junction of an young man and a woman accomplished in all ideal qualities. In its immeasurable height and depth no other form of it can bear camparision with it. The man becomes the idol of her heart, while the woman occupies his inner sanctuary, at whose altar he will never hesitate to make any sacrifice that may be required of him. Thus the two come to acquire the virtue of leading a life for the other, a virtue, as it grows, makes it possessor identify his or her interests with the interests of the righteous. 
From this form of the purest spiritual love, arises the necessity of acquiring wealth, not for filling his own wants, not for securing comforts and pleasures for his own self, but for keeping the object of his adoration, the offsprings of their love-union, the kith and kin of his heart, in un­mixed happiness and comfort. As the circle of those, whom he is thus bound by love and not by mere duty to support widens, as the number of those who lie under his protec­tion increases, ambition far wealth, power and distinction also increases, and this exercises his intelligence utmost and calls forth the play of all the innate powers of his mind. There can be no a greater incentive to stimulate the’ intellectual energies af man, and nothing can induce him to display his formidable heroic deeds more, than what offers him a stout resistance to the achievement of his objects. This consideration naturally leads the author to bring in the topic of war, since war is the only perilous kind of resistance and obstacle that a man of the highest responsi­bilities meets with in the path of his ambitious life. 
And lastly, when success has brought home to his mind, and to the mind of his partner, how full of cares, troubles, and untold miseries this earthly life is, to what great agony both the mind and body of a numberless beings human and animal are subjected by a few who seek their ease, comfort and happiness, the man and the woman, jointly direct their thoughts to the attinment of a bliss unattended by misery. Thus a fresh regeneration of their life commences in the purely spiritual atmosphere in which the aroma of divine love emanates from God, accord­ing to Thalkappiar, the ever resplendent intelligent principle, exhilarates their mind and transmutes their human love into divine. 
It is remarkable that in emphasizing the importance of turning their mutual human love to the love of God, the married couple is advised by our 81. Tholkappiar to strive after it conjointly. From this it appears that, at the time of Tholkappiar, the life of celibacy and asceticism was quite unknown. Even in the north it came into vogue only after­the rise of Buddhism and Jainism. In the pre-Buddhistic, that is, in the Vedic period, the life of the Aryan house­holder and the Aryan sage was similar to that of the Tamilian, but with this difference that, while in the former mere carnal pleasures reign supreme, in the latter an ideal and chaste love cast around its sweet and mellow light. 
On all these points I have expatiated to the best of my lights and have shown how the principles of life lid down 5000 years ago by 81. Tholkappiar in his immortal Tamil work Tholkappiam, are in striking and wonderful harmony with the truly physical, moral, intellectual, and religious requirements of advancing ffi:)dern civilisation. It is a great pleasure to me to acknowledge my gratitude to the whole modern Tamil world who, with but one strange exception, appreciated this my humble contribution to modern Tamil literature and spoke and wrote of it in very high terms. To the strange critic also, who is the type of the unprogressive conservative section, I express my grateful feelings for having given me an opportunity to defend my position more strongly than before, in the second and concluding parts. 
Pallavaram,	VEDACHALAM
The sacred order of Lover 
30th April, 1930.

paḻantamiḻk koḷkiyē civacamayam
1. paṇṭi nākarika makkaḷ
innilavulakam eṅkaṇum iruntu uyirvāḻntu varukiṉṟa palavēṟu makkaṭkūṭṭattāriṟ, paṇṭikkālan toṭṭu nākarikam uṭiyarāy vāḻntu varuvōr mikac cilarēyāvar. accilarāvār; ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, yūtar, cīṉar, pāracikar, āriyar, tamiḻar, mekcikar, perūviyar eṉpavarē yāvar. ivaruḷ ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, ācīriyar, yūtar eṉṉum aimperuṅkūṭṭattārum ivintiya nāṭṭukkup puṟampē mēṟkelliyil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷil iruntōr āvar; cīṉar pāracikar āriyar eṉpār iv intiya nāṭṭukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa mērumalip pakkattiliruntu teṟkum mēṟkuṅ kiḻukkuñ ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭipukuntu vikiṉōrāvar; tamiḻar eṉpār vaṭakkē imayamaliyiliruntu teṟkē kaṭalil amiḻntip pōṉa kumari nāṭṭiṉ elli muṭiya iruntōr āvar; mekcikar perūviyar eṉpār naṭu amerikkāvilum ataṟkuc ciṟitu teṟkēyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum iruntōr āvar. ippalavēṟu makkaṭkūṭṭattāriṉ nākarika vāḻkkiyellām iṟṟikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē naṭipeṟṟaṉa ākum. ivartam vāḻkkiyiṉ varalāṟukaḷi nām uḷḷapaṭi uṇaral vēṇṭumāyiṉ, ivarkaḷ paṇṭi
nāḷilē kaṭṭivitta kuvinta kōpuraṅkaḷum, nilattiṉkīḻp putinta araṇmaṉikaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ cuvarkaḷiluṅ kaḷimaṇ palakikaḷilum poṟittuvitta aracar varalāṟukaḷum, avarkaḷ iyaṟṟivitta nūlkaḷumē ataṟkuk karuviyāyiruntu utavukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ utavi koṇṭu ippaṇṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷi nām ārāyntu pārkkuṅkāṟ, cīṉar pāracikar āriyar eṉṉum mūṉṟu vakuppārit tavira maṟṟiyōriṉ nākarika vāḻkki ēṟakkuṟiya otta niliyiṉatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, yūtar eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu nākarika makkaḷ vakuppiṉaril ekupatiyariṉ nākarikavāḻkkiyē, ēṉi ellārtam nākarika vāḻkkiyiṉum mikka paḻimiyuñ ciṟappum uṭiyatāka nuvalap paṭukiṉṟatu. iṟṟikku eṇṇāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iv ekupatiyar araciyaṟ ṟuṟiyilum, oppuravu vāḻkkiyilum, camaya uṇarcciyilum, nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amippatilun, tirukkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭuvatilum, ēṉi nākarika makkaḷellāruṅ kaṇṭu tikikkattakka attaṉi tiṟamiyum aṟivum celvamum paṭittiruntaṉar eṉa avartam nākarika varalāṟṟiṉi naṉku ārāyntuṇarnta or āciriyar pirakcu pē (Brugsch Bey) pakarkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam eṇṇāyira āṇṭu kaṭku muṉṉamē nākarika vāḻkkiyil taliciṟantu niṉṟavarākac collappaṭutaṟku ēṟṟa meyccāṉṟukaḷ vāytta paṇṭi nākarika makkaḷ ekupatiyarit tavira vēṟevarumilli yeṉpatē varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷiṉ muṭipāyiṉum, ekupati nāṭṭil teṟalpaḵṟi (Der - el Bahri) eṉṉum ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷa aṣtāp (Hashto) araciyiṉ kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭirukkum ōviya eḻuttuk kalveṭṭukkaḷil ivekupatiyar teṉkaṭalakattuk kumarināṭṭilirunta pāṇṭi (Punt) tēyattiliruntu ceṉṟu nīlayāṟṟaṅkarikkaṭ kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu viḷakkamākac collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.1 ic ciṟanta paḻaṅ kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟu koṇṭum, iṅṅaṉamē, cālaṭi nāṭṭiṉkaṇ akaḻntu kaṇṭa cālapperiya araṇmaṉikaḷ kōyilkaḷiṟ kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ palakikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa varalāṟu kaḷāṟ pulaṉākun tamiḻppeyarkaḷ koṇṭum, avaṟṟōṭu uṭaṉakaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭut tēkkumarat tuṇṭaṅkaḷ iṟṟikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cālaṭiyaratu ūr eṉṉun talinakariṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa avvaraṇmaṉikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭami koṇṭum,2 iṟṟikku mūvāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa viviliyanūṟ pakutiyil mayiṟṟōki, akil mutalāṉa centamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭutal koṇṭum3 āriya moḻi irukkuvētattum paḻantamiḻmoḻit tol kāppiyattuṅ kāṇappaṭuṅ kuṟippukaḷ koṇṭum nam paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ iṟṟikkup pattāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarēyē nākarika vāḻkkiyil taliciṟantu niṉṟār
kaḷeṉpatum, attamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟic cālaṭi, ekupati mutalāṉa mēṉāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkut tamatu nākarika vāḻviṉi nāṭṭiya tamiḻvaḻi makkaḷē cālaṭiyarum ekupatiyarum avarkku iṉamāṉa ēṉi mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷum āvareṉpatum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, naṭu amerikkāvilun teṉ amerikkāvilum ēḻāyira āṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ uyirvāḻnta makkaḷun tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷē eṉpataṟku vēṇṭuñ cāṉṟukaḷ pulaṉāy varukiṉṟaṉa.4 iṅṅaṉan tamiḻnāṭṭilirunta makkaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷākavō, allatavarōṭum avar tam vāḻkkiyōṭun toṭarpuṭiyarākavō kāṇappaṭum ippaṇṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarika vāḻkkiyiṉ varalāṟukaḷ attaṉiyuñ ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya otta taṉmiyavāy iruttal peritum viyakkaṟpāla toṉṟām.

2. nākarikam
nākarikamāṉatu akamum puṟamuḷḷa irutaṉmip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ akanākarikamāṉatu aṉpu eṉṉum nekiḻnta iyaṟkiyiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu nikaḻvatu. maṟṟup, puṟanākarikamō celvam eṉṉum vaṉporuḷiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu nikaḻvatu. akattē naṭipeṟum aṉpoḻukkam mēṉmēl uram peṟṟu vaḷartaṟkup, puṟattēyuḷḷa celvam utaviceyyum muṟiyil oḻukum makkaḷiṭattē tāṉ, akanākarikamum puṟanākarikamum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu māṟukoḷḷātāy oruṅkottu, ammakkaḷukku immi maṟumi iṉpaṅkaḷi nirampa ūṭṭi, avar tammi maṅkāniliyiṟ poṅka vikkum. avvāṟu akavoḻukkamāṉa aṉpu vaḷarccikkut tuṇipuriyāmal, uyiriṉip puṉitappaṭuttum aṉpu ceṉṟavaḻiyē tāṉ cellāmal, ellā nilikaḷiṉum mēmpaṭṭa nilikkaṇ vikum aṉpukkut tāṉ aṭaṅki naṭavāmal, tāṉ perukutaṟku aṉpiṉi oru tuṇiyākap payaṉpaṭuttit, tāṉ muṉcelluñ celavukku aṉpiṉip piṉcella vittu, aṉpu niṟṟaṟku uriya uyarniliyil taṉṉi ēṟṟivittut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkuriya kīḻniliyil aṉpi iṟakki vittup, puṟanākarikap poruḷākiya celvamāṉatu taṉṉi maṟantu kūttāṭumāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam atu kūttāṭutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭukkum makkaḷiṭiya akanākarimum puṟanākarikamum oṉṟiṉoṉṟu muraṇi, avartam immi yiṉpaṅkaḷiyum peṟa vōṭṭāmal avarkkup peruntuṉpaṅkaḷi malipōṟ perukkic, cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil avartammi irunta iṭamun teriyāmal maṟiyavikkum. muṉniṟkavēṇṭiya aṉpu piṉniṟkavum, piṉniṟka vēṇṭiya celvam muṉniṟkavum muṟi māṟiṉamiyā laṉṟō tamiḻ makkaḷō ṭotta paḻam peruñ ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya ekupatiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, cālaṭiyar, acīriyar, āriyar, mekcikar, perūviyar mutalāṉa makkaṭpiriviṉar aṭiyōṭu māyntu pōyatōṭu, avar vaḻaṅkiya moḻikaḷum, avar vakutta eḻunilimāṭa nakaraṅkaḷum oruṅkē aḻin toḻintaṉa! avarellām avvāṟoḻiyavun, tamiḻmakkaḷ maṭṭum aṉṟu toṭṭu iṉṟukāṟum, av vuyarniliyiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu ciṟitu vaḻuviṉum, muṟṟum aṟṟoḻiyātu, ēṟakkuṟiyat tamatu paṇṭic ciṟappiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟa cāṉṟōr cilariyāyiṉum iṉṉum uṭiyarāy uyirpiḻittu niṟkiṉṟaṉar; avar vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻmoḻiyum ammoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa arumperu nūlkaḷum ēṉip paṇṭi moḻikaḷip pōl, ammoḻi nūlkaḷip pōl iṟavātu varavara uyirppēṟi ulavi varukiṉṟaṉa.

3. tolkāppiyam
ekupatiyar cālaṭiyar mutalāṉa paṇṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarika muṟikaḷi yuṇartaṟku arumperuñ cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittāṟpōlat, tamiḻppaḻa makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷi attuṇi uṟutiyāy aṟivikkum paḻiya meyccāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittilāmiyiṉ aṟiyāmip pēriruḷiṟkiṭantu tuṉpuṟṟa nām, mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkāṟum oḷi ciṟitum illāt tiṇiyiruḷiṟkiṭantu peritu varuntiya innilavukattu vaṭamuṉikkaṇ uḷḷa makkaḷ am muḻuviruṭ kālakkaḻiviṟ cuṭarntu pēroḷi vīci vāṉiṉkaṇ tulaṅkum puttapputiya oru perumiṉṉoḷiyiṉik kaṇṭu makiḻvatoppa, nammuṉṉōriṉ aṟivup peruveḷiyil aṟivoḷi vīcit tikaḻāniṉṟa tolkāppiyam1 eṉṉum muḻumutal nūliṉ kāṇṭakutōṟṟa viḷakkattiṉik kaṇṭu kaḷippeytutaṟku uriyēm allēmō!  maṟṟip paṇṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarika varalāṟukaḷi muṟṟum uṇartaṟkuk karuviyākāmal avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavē yuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāyk kiṭitta avar taṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟ poṟitta kalveṭṭukkaḷum pōṟ kuṟipāṭuṭiyataṉṟāy, nam aruntamiḻ makkaḷiṉ peruntiru nākarikavaralāṟu muṟṟumeṭuttu muṭiyakkūṟum muḻumatiyāy oḷirum nam tolkāppiya nūliṉ olkāp perumāṭciyiṉik kaṇṭu kaḷikkum tavam uṭiyēm allamō! iṟṟikku āṟāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē, ēṉi nākarika makkaḷellām perumpālum puṟanākarikac celva mayakkiṟ kiṭantu tammiyum uṇarāmal tām piṟavi
yeṭutta nōkkattiyum uṇarāmal tammiyum ulakattiyum paṭitta talivaṉiyum avaṉṟaṉ tiruvuḷakkuṟippiṉiyum uṇarāmal vāṇāḷi vīṇāḷāyk kaḻit aññāṉṟu, appuṟanākarika miṉukkiṉi akanākarika aṉpoḻukkap pēroḷiyiṉ muṉviḷakkamaṟac ceytu, nam mutumakkaḷip pēraṉpām iṉpap perukkiṟ paṭivittu oppuyarvillā meypperun tolkāppiya nūṟ ṟeyvamāṭciyiṉik kāṇṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaḷippeytutaṟkuriyam allamō! ittuṇicciṟanta tolkāppiyat teyva mutal nūl, puṟanākarikatti akanākarikattiṉkīḻ aṭakkic, cāyam aṟṟa celvap poruḷukku aṉpiṉ eḻil tulaṅku cāyam ēṟṟi, uyiraṟṟa eṟpuṭampu pōlvatākiya celvattiṟku aṉpeṉṉum uraṅkiḷar uyir pukuttip, puṟanākarikatti akanākarikattiṉvaḻi naṭatti, nam paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ oṇṭamiḻ koḷkiyiṉi āyntāyntu viḷakkiya arumperu vakimiyiṉi aṭivē ciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām.
aṭikkuṟippu
*1.	itaṉ kāla ārāycciṉi māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum ematu virinta nūliṟ kāṇka.

4. iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum
innilavulaka vāḻkkiyil makkaḷellārum aṭitaṟ kuriya uṟutipporuḷkaḷi iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṉum mūṉṟāka vakuttu ammūṉṟum piriviṉṟik kalanta aṉpiṉāl ūṭuruvi niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatu teḷivippār,
iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku
aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṉ	(kaḷaviyal 1)
eṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aruḷic ceytami peritum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpālatākum. tolkāppiyat tōṭotta paḻimivāynta irukku ecur cāmam mutaliya āriya nūlkaḷiṉātal, allatu ēṉippaḻiya makkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷiṉātal, iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṉum im mūṉṟum aṉpiṉ vaḻiyē aṭiyappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa nuvalap paṭāmiyiṉi uṟṟuṇarntu pārppavarkkē ammūṉṟum aṉpoṭu puṇarntu aṭiyappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa vaṟpuṟutturikkun tolkāppiyaṉār tam uripporuṇ mēṉmi naṉku viḷaṅkāniṟkum. tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻivanta āciriyaṉmāril taliciṟantavarāṉa teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
 aṉpiṉ vaḻiya tuyirnili aḵtilārkku
eṉputōl pōrtta uṭampu
eṉṟum,
 aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭittāyiṉ ilvāḻkkip
paṇpum payaṉum atu
eṉṟum aruḷic ceytamiyum, avarkkuppiṉ vanta civa
camayāciriyarum, civanāyaṉmārum, avar tamakkup piṉ iṟṟiñāṉṟu vari irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ īṟāka vanta civap periyārum ellām aṉpoḻukkattiṟkē talimi tantamiyum oru toṭarpupaṭuttu nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, toṉṟutoṭṭu itukāṟum nam tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vāḻkki yāṉatu aṉpoḻukkattiṉiyē accāṇiyāyk koṇṭu naṭipeṟuvateṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. itaṉic civa cittānta nūlākiya tirukkaḷiṟṟup paṭiyārum,
aṉpēeṉ aṉpēyeṉ ṟaṉpāl aḻutaraṟṟi
aṉpē yaṉpāka aṟivaḻiyum - aṉpaṉṟit
aṉpē tiyāṉañ civārccaṉikaḷ ceyyumavi
cāṟṟum paḻamaṉṟē tāṉ
eṉa iṟivaṉi nōkkic ceyyum vaḻipāṭukaḷ attaṉiyum uṇmi yaṉpiṉ puṟa nikaḻccikaḷākac ceyaṟpāla taṉi vaṟpuṟuttāniṟkiṉṟatu.

5. iṉpanili
iṉi, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār makkaḷaṭivataṟkuriya uṟutip poruḷkaḷ mūṉṟil iṉpam eṉpataṉi muṉvittuk kūṟiya nuṭpam iṉṉateṉa nām ārāyntaṟital vēṇṭum. piṟaviyeṭukkummuṉ tam viḻivu aṟivu ceyalkaḷ muḻutum maṟivuṇṭu tuṉpuṟṟum, aṉṉiyiṉ vayiṟṟil karuvāyp pukuntu piṟavi yeṭukkiṉṟa kālattu aṅkē mala nīrp pikaḷāl nerukkuṇṭu akaṭṭut tīyiṉāl vetumpip palavāṟu tuṉpuṟṟum, piṉṉar aṉṉi vayiṟṟi yakaṉṟu piḷḷiyāy piṟantu palvaki nōykaḷālun tammic cūḻa uḷḷār ceyyuṅ koṭumikaḷālum peritu tuṉpuṟṟum iṅṅaṉamākap piṟavikku varum muṉṉēyiruntu piṟaviyeṭuttu iḷamip paruvatti eytuṅkāṟum makkaḷ ellārum paṭuṅ koṭuntuṉpattiṟku ōr aḷavēyilli. ivvāṟu tuṉpat tilēyē paḻakivarum ēḻimakkaḷākiya nām iṉpattiṉ iyalpu iṉṉateṉa oru ciṟitāyiṉum paḻaki yaṟintilamāyiṟ pēriṉpappēṟṟi aṭitaṟku nampāl viruppam uṇṭākumō? uṇṭākātaṉṟē; ennēramun tuṉpattilēyē kiṭantuḻalum makkaḷil ettaṉipēr ivvulaka vāḻkkiyiṉi veṟuttu aiyō tammuyiric caṭutiyil māyttukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar! maṇṭivali vayiṟṟuvali iṭuppuvali mutaliya koṭu nōykaḷāl ōvātu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭippōriṟ paṟpalar tammuyirit tāmākavē māyttuk koḷḷutali aṭuttaṭuttuc ceytit tāḷkaḷāl aṟintu tikil koḷkiṉṟaṉar allamō! nalviṉi vayattāṟ cuviyāṉa uṇavukaḷi yuṇṭuṅ, kaṇṇārap pala kāṭcikaḷik kaṇṭum, iṉiya icikaḷ ariya virivurikaḷ kēṭṭum, arumperu nūlkaḷ kaṟṟu aṟivu teḷintum, naṟumaṇam aḷintum, ivvāṟellām aimpulaṉkaḷiṉ vāyilāka varum palatiṟa iṉpaṅkaḷi nukarntu makiḻtaliṉāṟṟāṉ, makkaḷuyir iḷamip paruvam eytum variyilātu innilavukattē nilavukiṉṟatu! nām piṟaviyeṭukkum muṉṉamē iṟivaṉ iv aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷit tarum palvakip paṇṭaṅkaḷi iṅku amittu vittilaṉāyiṉ, nām oru noṭip poḻutāyiṉum iṅkē uyirvāḻtal iyalumō? kūrntu pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ivvimpula nukarccipporuḷkaḷum vāyppāka naṉku amikkappaṭṭiruttaliṉālētāṉ palakōṭik kaṇakkāṉa palvaki makkaṭ piriviṉar tam uyirvāḻkkiyum in nilavulakattiṉkaṇ āṅkāṅku naṭipeṟā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉi aimpula nukarccikaḷāl varum iṉpattaḷavil makkaḷuyir amitipeṟuṅ kālam evvaḷavu? peṇmakkaḷāyiṉ maṅkipparuvam eytum variyilum, āṇmakkaḷāyiṉ iḷamip paruvam eytum variyilumō maṟṟu apparuvam vanta aḷavilō, atukāṟuṅ kāṇakkiṭiyā vēṟoru makiḻccik kiṭamām vēṟoru pututēyattik kaṇṭāloppap, putiyatōr iṉpa uṇarcci varappeṟṟu avviṉpattip peṟutalil iruvaruyirum peruvēṭkiyum perumuyaṟciyum uṭiyavākiṉṟaṉa. atuvariyil nukarntuvanta aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷellām, ippōtu tōṉṟāniṉṟa ipputiya iṉpattiṉmuṉ nillāvāy, ataṉiṟ ṟāḻntavāy, ataṉip peṟātavaḻi ataṉāl ēkkamuṟuvārkkut tuṉpan
tarum peṟṟiyavāy māṟiviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟu iḷamip paruvattil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārkkun tōṉṟuvatākiya iṉpa vuṇarcciyāṉatu, aḻaku aṟivu nalliyaṟkiyiṉāl untappaṭṭu eḻum aṉpiṉ vayamāki oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyārāy oruṅku poruntum āṇ peṇ makkaḷukku avviḻumiya kātaliṉpattiṉi ūṭṭi, avaratu vāḻkkiyit tūymi ceytu ataṉip peritum payaṉpaṭac ceyvatākum. ikkātaliṉpa uṇarcci tōṉṟutaṟku muṉṉellām piḷḷimip paruvattiluḷḷār tāntām peṟṟa aimpula iṉpap poruḷkaḷit tāntāmē nukarvatiṟ karuttuṭiya raṉṟit, tammō ṭotta maṟṟip piḷḷikaṭku avaṟṟi eḷitiṟkoṭār; tām nukarntu miñciya miccan tamakku vēṇṭātiruntālmaṭṭum, ataṉi ēṉic ciṟuvarkkuk koṭukka orōvorukāl maṉam oruppaṭuvar; eṉṟālun, tām nukarntu kaḻitta miccattiyum piṟarkkuk koṭukka maṉam iciyāta ciṟārē ulakiṟ perumpālār. ivvāṟākat, taṉṉalam oṉṟē karutum piḷḷimip
paruvan tāṇṭi, iḷamipparuvam pukuntu, kātaliṉpa uṇarcci vayattarāy ōrāṇmakaṉum oru peṇmakaḷum oruvarpā loruvar mikka aṉpu mītūrappeṟṟu oruṅku poruntiya kālan toṭṭō, kaṇavaṉāyiṉāṉ taṉnalaṅ karututal viṭṭut taṉ maṉiviyiṉ nalaṅ karutuvāṉāy, avaṭku vēṇṭum nukar poruḷkaḷellān tokuttuk koṭukkum poruṭṭu muyalum palvaki muyaṟcikaḷāl ulakattiṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭu kiṉṟāṉ; aṅṅaṉamē maṉiviyāyiṉāḷun taṉṉip pēṇuṅ karutti viṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉiyē taṉṉuyirākak karuti avaṉi ōmputalilun; tam aṉpiṉ cērkkiyāṟ piṟakkum iṉpak kuḻavikaṭkut tāṉ varintu vittavikaḷi yellāṅ koṭuttu avarik kaṇṇuṅ
karuttumāy pōṟṟi vaḷarttalilum aruḷoḻukka muṭiya aṟavōrkku vēṇṭuva koṭuttalum aṉpoḻukkamuṭiya aṟavōrkku vēṇṭuva koṭuttalum antaṇarik kāttalum tuṟaviṉari ēṟṟu avarkkut toṇṭu puritalum viruntu varuvāri ōmputalumākiya aṟampuri vāḻkkiyilun tali niṉṟu immaṇṇulaka vāḻkkiyiṉi viṇṇulaka vāḻkkiyeṉap puṉitamākkit tāṉum mikap payaṉ paṭukiṉṟāḷ. pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam āṇpeṇ eṉṉum irupālāruṅ kātaliṉ uṇarcci vayattarāy oruṅku iyintamiyālaṉṟō, avar tāmum iṉpuṟṟup piṟariyum iṉpuṟuttip, piṟavikku varāmal aruvaniliyil aṟiyāmi yāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭuk kiṭanta uyirkaḷ uruvaniliyil tampāṟ piḷḷikaḷāyp piṟavi yeṭuttu aṟivum iṉpamum peṟap pērutavipurintu avvāṟṟāl iṟivaṉṟaṉ aruṭceyaṟku ōr oppaṟṟa karuviyāyp payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ikkātaliṉpa uṇarcciyum ataṟkēṟṟa amippum makkaḷpāl illi yāyiṉ, ivvulakam eṅkē! ivvulakil naṭipeṟum oḻukkaṅkaḷ eṅkē! yār evarkku uṟavu! yār evarkkut tuṇi! ellām veṟum pāḻāy muṭiyumaṉṟō? evvāṟṟāl ārāyntu pārppiṉum ikkātaliṉpamāṉatu uṭampukku uyirpōlvatāy, iruḷniṟinta iravil iruḷakaṟṟum muḻumati pōlvatāyp, puṟpūṇṭukaḷ mutal niliyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum vilaṅku puḷ makkaḷ mutal iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum aṟivum muyaṟciyum peṟac cuṭaroḷi parappum ñāyiṟu pōlvatāy, aṟiyāmiyāṟ ciṉattāṟ poṟāmiyāl iṟumāppāl oruvarō ṭoruvar aḷavaḷāvutal illāta makkaḷi aḷavaḷāvap pukuttum iṟivaṉṟaṉ aruḷviḷakkam pōlvatāy mutaṉmi peṟṟu vayaṅkutal kaṇṭaṉaṟō tavattāṉ muṟṟuṇarvu vāynta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉa iṉpatti mutal nilikkaṇ vattu ōtuvārāyiṉar.
immutalāciriyarkkup piṉvanta tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ, ulakiyaloḻukkat tiṟku muṟṟum māṟāṉa putta camaṇa camayak koḷkikaḷāṟ ciṟitu ciṟitu īrppuṇṭu,
 ciṟappuṭi marapiṟ poruḷum iṉpamum
aṟattu vaḻippaṭūun tōṟṟam pōla
eṉa aṟattiṟkē mutalnili kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, tiruvaḷḷuva ṉārum aṟatti muṉ vittup poruḷi ataṉ piṉ vittu iṉpatti iṟutikkaṇ vittu nūl ceytārāyiṉum, iṉpatti nutaliyaṉṟi aṟañceyvāriyum poruḷ ceyvāriyum yāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilamākaliṉ, tolkāppiya ṉār iṉpatti mutalvitta muṟiyē makkaḷiyaṟkiyoṭu poruntuvatātal naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. tamatu kālappōkkiṉi yoṭṭit tiruvaruḷḷuvaṉār  aṟatti muṉvittu iṉpatti iṟutik kaṇ vittu nūliyaṟṟappukuntaṉarāyiṉum, iṉpatti vāyilākakkoṇṭaṉṟi aṟaṉum illaṟan tuṟavaṟam eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, kātaliṉpattiṉ vaḻippaṭuvatākiya illaṟattiyē mutaṟkaṇ vittu nūl aruḷic ceytaṉar. avvāṟṟāl āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa muṟiyē naṉmuṟiyeṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārkkum uṭam pāṭātal peṟutum. iḵtu avar illaṟaviyaliṉ mutalatikārattil, 
 aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭittāyiṉ ilvāḻkkip 
paṇpum payaṉum atu
eṉa aṉpi muṉvittu aṟaṉi ataṉpiṉ vittut teḷittōtiyatu koṇṭum iṉitu peṟappaṭum.

6. iṉpam aṉpiṉāl nikaḻvatu : kaṟpu
ittuṇic ciṟantatākiya iṉpa vāḻkki aṉpiṉāl untappaṭṭē naṭipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉa mutal āciriyar valiyuṟuttiyavāṟu pōlavē, teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvarum,
 aṉpuṟ ṟamarnta vaḻakkeṉpa viyakat
tiṉpuṟṟār eytuñ ciṟappu
eṉṟu uṭampāṭṭāṉum,
 aṉpakat tillā uyirvāḻkki vaṉpāṟkaṇ
vaṟṟaṉ marantaḷirt taṟṟu
eṉṟu etirmaṟiyāṉum aṉpiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉi viḷaṅka vittatu kāṇka. maturik kaṭiccaṅka kālattukkum muṉṉē paṇṭic centamiḻk kālattirunta kuṉṟam pūtaṉār eṉṉum nallicip pulami malinta cāṉṟōrun tām murukavēḷmēṟ pāṭiya irunilan tuḷaṅkāmi eṉṉum paripāṭalilum,
nāṉmaṟi virittu nallici viḷakkum
vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ ciṟantatu
kātaṟ kāmaṅ kāmattuc ciṟantatu
eṉṟu kātaliṉpattiṉ māṭci terittu, avviṉpa nukarcci iṟivaṉatu taṇparaṅkuṉṟattiṉkaṇ nikaḻumāṟṟiṉ vittu ataṉi virittup pāṭiṉār.
pāruṅkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ulakiya loḻukkattiṟkum iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭip pēṟṟiṟkum āṇivērpōl iruppatākiya iv iṉpavoḻukkamāṉatu meyyaṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkumāyiṉ appōtutāṉ atu kātaliṉpam eṉa uyarttup pēcappaṭumeṉpatum, aḵtu aṅṅaṉam aṉpiṉvaḻi naṭavātāyiṉ iḻinta kāmam eṉa iḻittup pēcappaṭumeṉpatum nam centamiḻt teyva āciriyar ellārkkum otta muṭipā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl ōrāṇmakaṉum oru peṇmakaḷun tammil oruvari yoruvar uyirpōṟ karuti nirampiya aṉpāl oḻukiṉālaṉṟip, peṇpālārkkuk kaṟpoḻukkam nilipeṟātu kaṟpoḻukkam nilipeṟatāyiṉ naṉmakkaḷip peṟutalum uḷatākātu; ulakil oruvarōṭoruvar amitiyāyiruntu vāḻāmaṟ pakimiyuñ ciṉamuṅ koṇṭa allal uḻappar; ataṉāṟ ṟeyva nampikkiyun teyva vaṇakkamum illiyāyp
pōm; eṅkuṅ kuṭiyuṅ koliyum koṭumiyum malintu makkaḷ vāḻkkiyāṉatu ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc citaṟiviṭum. ittakiya kuṭikēṭāṉa ceyal ruṣiyā mutalāṉa mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ paravi aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷi niṉippiṉuṅ koṭiya tuṉpattiṟ paṭuttut tēyttuvarum niliyiṉic ciṟitāyiṉum aḻuntiyaṟiyavallārkkē, nam centamiḻt teyva āciriyar vaṟpuṟuttic colliya kātaliṉpa vāḻkkiyiṉ viḻuppam pulaṉākāl niṟkum. maṟṟuk kātalaṉpiṉvayamāki maṇaṅ kūṭiṉārkkō oruvari viṭṭup piṟaroruvar mēl uḷḷañ cellātākaliṉ, avarkkuk kaṟpoḻukkan tāṉākavē nilipeṟum; avarkkuk kaṟpoḻukkattiṉik kaṟpikka vēṟevarumē vēṇṭappaṭār. avar, tām kātal koṇṭa aṉpari yaṉṟip piṟarevariyuṅ karutamāṭṭāmiyāl, tam karuttaṟiyāmal tammip piṟaroruvarkku maṇam pukunta evarēṉum muṉikuvarāyiṉ, avarkkut tamatuḷḷak karuttip pulappaṭuttit tamatu kaṟpoḻukkattiṉi niliniṟuttik koḷvar; atuvum vāyātāyiṉ tamatuyiriyē tuṟapparaṉṟit tamatu kaṟpiṟ ciṟitum vaḻuvār; tam kātalar iṟantupaṭiṉ tāmum uyir tāṅkār. ittaṉmiyarākiya paṇṭit tamiḻ mātariṉ kaṟpoḻukka māṭciyiṉip, paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ pōnta cila pāṭṭukkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkutum; mutaliṟ kalittoki eṉṉum ariya caṅka nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum oru ceyyuḷi iṅkeṭutturippām:
cuṭarttoṭīi! kēḷāy! teruvilnām āṭum
maṇaṟciṟṟil kāliṟ citiyā aṭicciya
kōti parintu varippantu koṇṭōṭi
nōtakka ceyyuñ ciṟupaṭṭi mēlōrnāḷ
aṉṉiyum yāṉum iruntēmā, illirē!
uṇṇunīr vēṭṭēṉ eṉavantāṟ kaṉṉi
aṭarpoṟ cirakattāl vākkic cuṭariḻāy
uṇṇunīr ūṭṭivā veṉṟāḷ, eṉayāṉum
taṉṉi yaṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉ maṟṟeṉṉi
vaḷimuṉki paṟṟi naliyat, terumantiṭṭu
aṉṉāy ivaṉoruvaṉ ceytatukāṇ eṉṟēṉā,
aṉṉi yalaṟip paṭartarat, taṉṉiyāṉ
uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ eṉṟōṉā, aṉṉiyun
taṉṉip puṟampuaḻittu nīva, maṟṟeṉṉik
kaṭikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉpōl nōkki nakikkūṭṭañ
ceytāṉ akkaḷvaṉ makaṉ.
taṉṉip piṟaṉoruvaṉukku maṇam poruttak karutit taṉ peṟṟōr ceyyum ēṟpāṭukaḷik kaṇṭu tikil koṇṭavaḷāṉa aḻaku mikka orukaṟpuṭi maṅki, tāṉ oruvaṉ mēṟ kātalkoṇṭirukkuṅ kuṟipput taṉ tōḻikkup pulaṉāmpaṭi collutali icceyyuḷ eṭuttiyampukiṉṟatu; itukēṭṭut taṉ taliviyiṉ kātaṟ kuṟippaṟinta tōḻi avaṭaṉ peṟṟōrkku ataṉi aṟivittu avaḷ kātalitta iḷiñaṉukkē avaḷi maṇaṅkūṭṭuvaḷ. itaṉi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉpar poruḷilakkaṇattil, icceyyuṭ poruḷ varumāṟu :-
viḷaṅkā niṉṟa toṭiyaṇinta tōḻī! eṉmaṉattuḷḷataṉik kēṭpāyāka! teruvilē nām maṇalāṟ camittu viḷiyāṭuñ ciṟiya vīṭṭiṉik kālāl eṟṟi aḻittum, namatu kūntaliṟ cerukiya pūmāliyi aṟuttum, varinta namatu pantiṉi eṭuttukkoṇṭōṭiyum nām maṉam nōvattakka kuṟumpukaḷic ceyyum antac ciṟu paṭṭippiyaṉ, muṉ oru nāḷ nam aṉṉiyum yāṉum vīṭṭiṉkaṇ iruntēmāka, empāṟpōntu, vīṭṭil uḷḷīrē! uṇṇappaṭun taṇṇīrkkāka viṭāy koṇṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu collivantu niṉṟaṉukku, aṉṉiyāṉavaḷ eṉṉi nōkkic cuṭariḻāy! poṟṟakaṭṭāṟ ceyta karakattāl uṇṇunīri mukantu ceṉṟu vārttu ataṉi avaṉ parukacceytuvā eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṟavē yāṉum avaṉip paṭṭip piyaṉ eṉṟu niliyāmalē aṉṉi coṉṉapaṭiyē ceyya avaṉṟaṉpāṟ ceṉṟēṉ; āṉāl avaṉ vaḷiyal aṇinta eṉatu muṉ kiyiṉip piṭittiḻuttu vampu ceyyavē, yāṉ maṉam maruṇṭu aṉṉāy! ït‹ xUt‹ brŒjij¥ ghuhŒ! eṉṟu kūviṉōṉāka, uṭaṉē aṉṉi alaṟik koṇṭu eṉpāl varutalum, caṭutiyil yāṉ maṉanteḷintu ivaṉ uṇṇunīrip parukumpōtu vikkivaruntiṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉ ceytataṉi maṟittu moḻintēṉ; atu kēṭṭu aṉṉiyum avaṉ mutukip palakālun taṭaviṉāḷ; appōtu eṉṉik kaṭikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉ pōl nōkki, makiḻccikku ētuvāṉa maṉattik kaḷvaṉākiya avviḷiñaṉ muṉṉamē ceytaṉaṉ; itaṉi aṟivāyāka!
pārmiṉkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷē! kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta iḷiya tamiḻ maṅki orutti taṉṉiḷamik kālattil nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷil vittut tāṉ ōr iḷiñaṉmēṟ kātal koṇṭamiyiṉi evvaḷavu nuṭpamāka, evvaḷavu tiṟamiyāka, evvaḷavu maṉakkavarcciyuṇṭākat tāṉkoṇṭa akkātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkattiṟkup paḻutuvārāmaṟ kūṟukiṉṟāḷ! tāṉ ētum aṟiyāp pētip peṇṇāy irunta kālattilēyē taṉ uḷḷam ōriḷiñaṉpāl aṉpiṟ patintamiyum, tāṉ iḻitta maṇal vīṭṭiṉik kālāl eṟṟiyun taṉ kūntaliṟ cerukiya māliyi aṟuttun tāṉ āṭum pantiṉi eṭuttuk koṇṭōṭiyum avaṉ tāṉ varuntattakka kuṟumpukaḷ ceytum, akkuṟumpukaḷ attaṉiyun taṉpāl vitta aṉpiṉālēyē avaṉ ceytamiyum, ataṉāl akkuṟumpukaḷ taṉmaṉam nōvacceytaṉapōṟ ṟōṉṟiṉum uṇmiyil avi taṉṉi nōvac ceyyāmiyum, ciṟupaṭṭi eṉa avaṉittāṉē cuṭṭuñ collē avaṉpāl taṉakkuḷḷa aṉpiṉ aṟikuṟiyātalum, viṭāy koṇṭu vantāṉeṉiṉum iḷiñaṉāṉa oruvaṟkut tāṉ taṉiyē ceṉṟu viṭāy taṇittal nāṇuṭiya peṇṭaṉmikku ēlāmiyiṉ aṉṉiyun tāṉum iruntakkāl avaṉ viṭāyttu vantāṉeṉṟamiyum, aṅṅaṉam aṉṉiyōṭu iruntaviṭattum aṉṉiyiṉ ēvutalālēyē tāṉ nīreṭuttu avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟumiyum, avaṉ taṉpāl vitta kātaṟkiḻamiyāl taṉ kiyip piṭittu vampuceyyiṉum ataṟku eḷitilē uṭaṉpaṭṭiruttal accamum nāṇamum iyaṟkiyē vāynta taṉ peṇṭaṉmikku ēlāmiyiṉ ataṟkiciyātu taṉ aṉṉiyik kūviyaḻittamiyum, aṉṉi arukuvantatum atupaṟṟi avaṉi avaḷ ciṉavāmip poruṭṭu avaṉ uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ eṉa avaṉ ceyta kuṟṟatti avaṉmēluḷḷa kātalāl tāṉ maṟittu moḻintamiyum, aṉṉiyum avaṉṟaṉ eḻil nalaṅ kaṇṭu avaṉ vikkutal poṟāyāy avaṉṟaṉ mutukiṉittaṭavikkoṭuttā ḷeṉṟamiyum, appōtu tāṉ ceytataṉi oru kuṟumpāka niṉintu avaṉ taṉṉik kaṭikkaṇṇāṟ pārttuc ciṉantamiyum elm av aṟivuṭik kaṟparaci ettuṇi nayamākat, taṉ uyarnta peṇṭaṉmikku ettuṇi icivāka, iṅṅaṉañ ciṉṉañ ciṟiya paruvam mutalē tāṉkoṇṭa kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkam ettuṇic ciṟantateṉpatu pulaṉākak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟāḷ attuṇi viḻumiya kātaṟ kaṟpuṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻi vanta nam iv varumiyiṉik kaṉaviṉum naṉaviṉum niṉintu niṉintu viyakkaṟpāla mallamō!
ittuṇic ciṟanta paṇṭit tamiḻmātariṉ kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukkatti eṭutturikkum it tamiḻcceyyuḷ vaḻakkum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatā yirukkiṉṟatu. vāṉmati maṇṭila ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉum mēluyarntu celluṅ kūṭa kōpura māḷikikaḷik kaṟpittu, nēriṟ ceṉṟu pārttāl oru cila kuṭicikaḷē yuḷḷa ōr ūril akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ uḷavāka maṉakkōṭṭi kaṭṭi, akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ mēlaṭukku kaḷil uḷḷavarāka mātarkaḷi irutti ammātariṉ muṭimutalaṭikāṟum uḷḷa ovvor uṟuppukaḷiyum paṟpala ceyyuṭkaḷāl aḻaku paṭuttippāṭi, iṅṅaṉamē iṉṉum illāta poykaḷi yellām ērāḷamāyp puṉintu kaṭṭic collum ikkālattār ceyyuḷvaḻak keṅkē! uḷḷavāṟē eṭuttiyampic, cintum piḻipaṭā teḻutiya ōviyam pōlvatākiya paḻantamiḻp pulavariṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṅkē! ivviraṇṭiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟu pāṭṭiṉik kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷōṭu ikkalittokic ceyyuḷic ciṟitu oppiṭṭu nōkkuvīrkkaḷāyiṉ naṉkaṟivīrkaḷ. atu niṟka.

7. tamiḻ mātariṉ kaṟpu
iṉip, paṇṭik kātaṟ kaṟpoḻukka mātar taṅ kaṇavar iṟantupaṭṭa ñāṉṟē, avartam piriviṉi āṟṟārāy ēṅki uyirtuṟanta nikaḻccikaḷum pala. ivvāṟu nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcciyiṉip puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyiṟ pōnta,
 ōruyirāka uṇarka uṭaṉ kalantārk
kīruyi reṉpar iṭiteriyār - pōril
viṭaṉēntum vēlōṟkum veḷvaḷiyi ṉāṭkum
uṭaṉē ulanta tuyir
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil ittakiya nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻntamiyāl avar avaṟṟiṟku mūtāṉantam eṉap peyariṭṭu, ataṉ ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiṉār, taṉ kātaṟkaṇavaṉ kōvalaṉi iḻanta kaṇṇaki paṭṭa tuyariṉi niṉippiṟ kaṉṉeñcamuṅ kariyu maṉṟō? avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉik koli ceyvitta pāṇṭiyaṉukku aṟivu teruṭṭip patiṉāṉkām nāḷ taṉṉuyiriṉit tuṟantu taṉ kaṇavaṉuyiroṭu vāṉulakuceṉṟa āṟṟaruṅ kāṭciyiṉik kuṟavaruṅ kaṇṭaṉa rallarō?
iṉit, taṉkaṇavaṉ pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ māynta ñāṉṟu avaṉip pirinta tuṉpam poṟāḷāy avaṉṟaṉ kātaṉ maṉivi peruṅkōppeṇṭu tīppāyac ceṉṟa vaḻi, avvāṟu ceytalākāteṉa vilakkiya cāṉṟōrkku avaḷ,
 palcāṉṟīrē! palcāṉṟīrē!
celkeṉac collā toḻikeṉa vilakkum
pollāc cūḻccip palcāṉṟīrē!
aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy vāḷpōḻn taṭṭa
kāḻpōl nalviḷar naṟuney tīṇṭātu
aṭiyiṭik kiṭanta kipiḻi piṇṭam
veḷḷeṭ cāntoṭu puḷippeytu aṭṭa
vēḷi venti valci yākap
paraṟpey paḷḷip pāyiṉṟu vatiyum
uyavaṟ peṇṭirēm allēm mātō;
peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭ ṭīmam
numakku aritākuka tilla; emakku em
peruntōṭ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa, arumpaṟa
vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmari
naḷḷirum poykiyun tīyum ōraṟṟē
eṉṟu ceyyuḷākavē viṭi kūṟiya nikaḻcci nam tamiḻ naṅkiyāriṉ kātalaṉ piṉ māṭciyiṉi malimēl vitta viḷakkuppōl ulakeṅkum viḷaṅkac ceykiṉṟataṉṟō?
ivvāṟākat tam uyiriyum oru turumpākak karuti nantam tamiḻp perumāṭṭimār tam kātalar maṭinta vaḻittāmum uṭaṉ maṭiyumāṟu avari avvāṟu ēviyatu etu avar taṅ koḻunar pāl vitta oppuyarvaṟṟa kātalaṉ paṉṟō? eṉavē, mātarkkuc ciṟanta kaṟpoḻukkamāvatu avartaṅ kātalaṉpiṉ payaṉāy avarpāl iyaṟkiyil uḷatāvatē
yaṉṟip, piṟarkku añciyum piṟar kūṟam aṟivuri kēṭṭum varuvataṉṟeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō iṅṅaṉam iyaṟki yoḻukkamāy varaṟpālatākiya kaṟpiṟkuk kātalaṉpu oṉṟumē ciṟanta ētuvāyirutlip pala paṭiyālum makkaḷiyaṟkiyil vittu ārāyntu kaṇṭamiyālaṉṟō āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
 iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku,
aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi
eṉṟu ōtiyaruḷiṉār, ammutalāciriyar vaḻivanta tiruvaḷḷuvar mutalāṉa cāṉṟōrum aṅṅaṉamē aḵtu aṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkaṟpāṟṟeṉṟu paṇṭu toṭṭu iṉṟu kāṟum valiyuṟutti vantirukkiṉṟaṉa rallarō?

8. iññāṉṟi maṉi vāḻkki
āṉāl, ippōtu naṭipeṟum maṉivāḻkki ettaṉmiyāy irukkiṉtu? aḵtu ulakiya loḻukkattiṟku uyirpōlvatākiya kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻiccolkiṉṟatā? illi, illi, ciṟitumēyilli. cātivēṟṟumi yeṉṉum koṭiyatūkkukkayiṟāṉatu kātalaṉpiṉ kaḻutti iṟukkiviṭṭatu; kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟantu maṟuvaṟṟamatipōl viḷaṅkat takkavarāṉa nam peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ kaṟpoḻukkatti nilikulittu ataṉip paḻipāvaṅkaḷāl mūṭiviṭṭatu? entap peṇmakaḷāvatu tāṉ kātalitta iḷiñaṉi maṇaṅkūṭa iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉaḷā? eḷḷaḷavum illiyē, ēṉ? oru cātikkuḷḷē tāṉ, orupatu vīṭukaḷē yuḷḷa ōr iṉattilē tāṉ avaḷ oruvaṉi maṇakkavēṇṭum. avaḷ kayalmīṉiyotta kaṇṇaḻakiyā yiruntāleṉṉa! kaṇkuruṭāṉa oruvaṉit tavira vēṟu maṇamakaṉ taṉ iṉattiṟ kiṭittilaṉāyiṉ avaḷ avaḷiyētāṉ maṇantu tīral vēṇṭum! avaḷ muttuk kōttālotta pallaḻakiyāy iruntāleṉṉa! taṉ pāḻum iṉattil oru pokki vāykkiḻavaṉit tavira vēṟumaṇamakaṉ kiṭittilaṉāyiṉ, avaḷ avaṉiyētāṉ maṇantutīral vēṇṭum! avaḷ palakali kaṟṟuk kalviyaṟivilum ici pāṭuvatilum nuṇṇaṟivilum civanēya aṭiyār nēyaṅkaḷiluñ ciṟanta kaṭṭaḻakiyā yiruntāleṉṉa! iṟumāppē kuṭikoṇṭa taṉciṟumākkuṭiyiṟ kalviyirunta mūliyē kaṇṭaṟiyātavaṉum, pāṭṭuppāṭa vāyit tiṟantāl ōṭṭamāy vara vaṇṇāṉi varuvippāṉum, aṟivi ōṭṭiviṭṭa veṟumūḷi yuṭiyāṉuṅ, kaṟuppaṇṇaṉ maturivīraṉ māriyammaṉ mutalāṉa veṟukkattakka pēykaṭku ūṉuṅkaḷḷumpaṭittuk kuṭittu veṟippāṉumākiya oruvaṉit tavira vēṟu maṇamakaṉ kiṭittilaṉāyiṉ, avaṉ taṉ cātiyiviṭṭu vēṟu cātiyiṟ kalakkalākāmiyiṉ, akkallāk kayavaṉiyē kaṇavaṉāyk koḷḷal vēṇṭum! ā! poruḷaṟṟa ic cātivēṟṟumik koṭumiyāl nam arumip peṇmaṇikaḷ paṭuntuyar maliyiṉum peritō! aṉṟik kaṭaliṉum peritō! aṉṟi ñālattiṉum peritō! ivvaḷavutāṉ eṉṟu kūṟal em oru nāvāl iyalātu!
pala nalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟanta or āṇmakaṉum aṅṅaṉamē taṉ cātiyi viṭṭup piṟa cātiyiṟ kalattal ākāmiyāl nalañ ciṟitumillā oru pōtip peṇṇi maṇantu tuṉpuṟa vēṇṭiyavaṉā yiruntālum, avaṉatu nili oruciṟanta peṇṇiṉ tuṉpa nilipōl attuṇik koṭitaṉṟu.
ēṉeṉṟāl, avaṉ tāṉ vēṇṭiyapaṭi vēṟu makaḷirik kūṭikkaḷittālum, avaṉi mikutiyāyk kuṟṟañ colvār illi. āṉāl, nam arumip peṇmakkaḷiṉ niliyō attakiya taṉṟē! avarkaḷ yārippārttāluṅ kuṟṟam, yāroṭu pēciṉālum ataṉiluṅ kuṟṟam, appuṟañcolla vēṇṭuvatu yātuḷatu? vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷuṅ kūṭat tān tām virumpiya tuṇiyiyē kūṭi iṉitu vāḻāniṟka, āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaṭ piṟaviyiṟ piṟantu aṟiviyum aḻakiyum naṟkuṇattiyuṅ kaṇṭu pārāṭṭik kātalaṉpu koḷḷattakka āṇ peṇ makkaḷ maṭṭum iṭiyē pukutta cātivēṟṟumi yeṉṉuṅ koliñaṉāl veṭṭiyaḻikkappaṭṭuk kātalaṉpu eṉṉum uyir ōṭippōṉa piṉ, iḻinta kāmappiṇa vāḻkkiyiṟ kālaṅkaḻikkumpaṭi kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭāl, itaṉiṉum vēṟu koṭuṅ koli uḷatō! kūṟumiṉkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷē! ulakiya loḻukkattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāy niṟkuṅ kātalaṉpik kīḻaṟuttuviṭṭāl, ataṉ mēl niṟkum avvulakiyal vāḻkki kīḻ viḻuntu nuṟuṅkā toḻiyumō! iṅṅaṉam makkaḷ vāḻkkiyi vērilēyē aṟuttuk kolipurivōr, aruḷoḻukkattiyum aṉpoḻukkattiyumē valiyuṟuttum nam teyva āciriyarāl vakukkappaṭṭa nam civa camayattiṟku uriyarāvarō! coṉmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē!

9. kārikkālammiyār maṉiyaṟam
nam arumic civacamayamāṉatu kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻi naṭakkun tūya vāḻkkiyiṉiyē vēṇṭuvataṉṟi, ataṉik koli ceyyuñ cātivēṟṟumi vāḻkkiyiṉic ciṟitum vēṇṭuvataṉ ṟeṉpataṟku nam civa nāyaṉmār varalāṟukaḷē cāṉṟātal ciṟitu kāṭṭutum. kārikkāl ammiyār eṉṉuñ civap peṇmaṇiyāriṉ varalāṟṟi muṉṉar ārāyvām. ivar teyvattaṉmi vāynta naṅkiyārātaluṭaṉ, tamiḻmoḻik kaṭaliyum ārapparuki ataṉkaṇ ariyaperiya ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷuḷ iyaṟṟun tiṟam vāyntavar. aḻakilō kival kammiyaṉ tiruttic camitta arumpeṟaṟpoṟpāviyi oppavar. ciṉṉañ ciṟu paruvam mutalē civapirāṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇṇuñ civaṉaṭiyār pālum mikka aṉpu pūṇṭavar. ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oru teyvamaṅkikku ēṟṟa kaṇavaṉi avar tāmē terintu kātalittal vēṇṭumaṉṟip, piṟar avarkkēṟṟa oruvaṉit teriya māṭṭuvārallar. aṅṅaṉa miruntum avartañ cuṟṟattār tam vaṇikamarapiṉāṉa oruvaṉik koṇarntu avarkkuk kaṇavaṉāka maṇam porutti viṭṭaṉar. avvāṟu maṇañ ceyvikkappaṭṭa avaṉ ammiyār tam arumikaḷi muṉṉarē yaṟintu avaṟṟiṟku ēṟpak kātalaṉpu pūṇṭu naṭakkat takka māṭci vāyntavaṉ allaleṉpatu, avaratu teyvat taṉmiyi aṟinta vaḷavil avaṉ avarit toṭutaṟku añciyiruntu, piṉṉarc cilanāṭkaḷil avar aṟiyāmē pirintu vēṟoru nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkē vēṟoruttiyi maṇantu koṇṭiruntamiyāṉum, atu terinta ammiyāriṉ uṟaviṉar avari oru civikiyil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu avaṉirunta ūrkkuppōṉa kāliyil avaṉ taṉ iraṇṭām maṉivi makkaḷuṭaṉ etirpōntu ammiyāri maṉiviyāka niliyāmal teyvamāka niṉintu avartan tiruvaṭikaḷil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉamiyāṉum naṉku peṟappaṭum. ammiyārum avaṉpāṟ kātalaṉpu nikaḻappeṟāmaṟ kaṇavaṉeṉṟakaṭamiyi niṉintu oruvāṟu aṉpupūṇṭoḻukiṉa reṉpataṟku. avaṉ taṉṉi vaṇaṅki niṉṟa kāliyil avaṉ poruṭṭut tāṅkiya aḻakiya uṭampiṉ vaṭivit tacip poti eṉa veṟuttuk kūṟi ataṉi māṟṟip pēy vaṭivu taṉakkut tarumāṟu avar iṟivaṉi vēṇṭiṉamiyē cāṉṟām; iḵtu āciriyar cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷ,
īṅkivaṉ kuṟitta koḷki ituiṉi ivaṉuk kākat
tāṅkiya vaṉappu niṉṟa tacip poti kaḻittiṅkuṉpāl
āṅkunariṉ tāḷkaḷ pōṟṟum pēyvaṭi vaṭiyē ṉukkup
pāṅkuṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟu paramaṉip paravi niṉṟār
eṉak kūṟamāṟṟāl teḷippaṭum. kātalaṉpu eṉpatu ōrāviṟku irukōṭu tōṟṟiṉāṟpōla iruvaruyirum ōruyirāy īruṭampiṉ niṉṟu iyiyum perukiya tūya aṉpākum. iḵtu āciriyar nakkīraṉār iṟiyaṉārākap poruḷuriyiṟ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāril,
kākat tirukaṇṇiṟ koṉṟē maṇikalan tāṅkiruvar
ākattuḷ ōruyir kaṇṭaṉam yāmiṉṟi yāviyumām
mēkat toruvaṉ irumpoḻi lampala vaṉmaliyil
tōkikkun tōṉṟaṟkum oṉṟāy varumiṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷē
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytamiyāṉum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. ippeṟṟit
tākiya kātalaṉpu kārikkālammiyārkkum avar taṅ kaṇavaṉārkkum nikaḻappeṟāmiyāl, avartañ cērkki veṟṟuṭampiṉ cērkkiyā yiruntē yallāmal, aṉpāl uruki oṉṟupaṭṭa uyiriṉ cērkkiyāy iruntatiṉṟu. ammiyārip pōlavē avaṉuṅ kalittamiḻp pulamiyil nirampic civanēya aṭiyār nēyaṅkaḷilum mikkiruntaṉa ṉāyiṉ, avaṉ avarkkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉāka, avartañ cērkki uyiriṉ cērkkiyēyā yiruntirukkum. avvāṟākāmikkuk kāraṇam ammiyārē tamakkut takkāṉoruvaṉit teriyātirukka, avartañ cuṟṟattār avviruvariyalpum āyntāyntu pārāmal avar tammip piṇittu viṭṭamiyē yākum.

10. cuntarartam kātalaṉpiṉ vāḻkki
iṉik, kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟanta iṉpavāḻkki iṭiniṉṟa cuṟṟattāriṉ muyaṟciyāṟ citivupaṭṭuk kārikkālammiyārkku iṉitu vāyātu pōṉami pōlavē, cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉārkkuṅ kātal maṉivāḻkki naṉku vāyātu pōyirukkum; āṉāl, ellām palla iṟivaṉ avarpāl mikka irakkampūṇṭu avarkku aṅṅaṉam nēra voṭṭāmal taṭiceytaruḷiṉaṉ. pāruṅkaḷ! cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ cuṟṟattārum, avartam pēraḻaku aruntamiḻp pulami civanēyam mutaliyavikaḷi āḻntāyntu pārttu, avartam teyva māṭcikku otta oru maṇamakaḷit terin
teṭuttavarallar; tamatu civa annataṇamarapil tāmvēṇṭiya oru peṇṇit terintu koṇṭu avarkku avaḷi maṇamporuttat tuvaṅkiṉar. atu kaṇṭa pēraruṭ kaṭalāṉa civapirāṉ ōr antaṇa mutiyōṉāy vantu, accuṟṟattār kūṭṭiya maṇam naṭipeṟāvāṟu taṭiceytu, avarit tiruveṇṇey nallūrkku īrttup pōy āṭkoṇ ṭaruḷiṉar. ataṉpiṟ cuntara mūrttikaḷ iṟivaṉpāl aṉpu mītūrappeṟṟup, pala tirukkōyilkaḷi vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭu, tiruvārūrkku vanta kāliyiṟ civapirāṉ tirukkōyilil āṭal pāṭalkaḷākiya tiruttoṇṭu ceytu koṇṭirunta iḷanaṟuṅ kaṉṉit teyva naṅkiyārāṉa paravinācciyārik kaṇṭu uḷḷamum uyirum uruki yoruṅkē avarpāṟ patiyap peṟṟārāyk kaḻiperuṅ kātalaṉpu koṇṭu,
 kaṟpakattiṉ pūṅkompō kāmaṉṟaṉ peruvāḻvō
poṟpuṭiya puṇṇiyattiṉ puṇṇiyamō puyalcumantu
viṟkuvaḷi pavaḷamalar matipūtta virikkoṭiyō
aṟputamō civaṉaruḷō aṟiyēṉeṉ ṟaticayittār
aṅṅaṉamē avarik kaṇṭa paravi nācciyārum itaṟku muṉ tām kaṇṭaṟiyāta oru teyvakkātal koḷḷappeṟṟu,
 muṉṉēvan tetirtōṉṟum murukaṉō perukoḷiyāṟ
ṟaṉṉēril māraṉō tārmārpiṉ viñciyaṉō
miṉṉērceñ caṭiyaṇṇal meyyaruḷpeṟ ṟuṭiyavaṉō
eṉṉēeṉ maṉantirinta ivaṉyārō eṉaniṉintār
pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! itteyvap perumāṉum perumāṭṭiyum oruvari yoruvar kātalittu, akkātalaṉpiṉ vayamāy uṇarvu celluṅkāluñ civaperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷē ivvāṟu uruvukoṇṭu tōṉṟiyatu pōlum eṉak karuti viyakkiṉṟār! teyvatti maṟantu, uyarnta nōkkaṅkaḷi maṟantu iḻinta ūṉuṭampiṉ cērkkiyilēyē niṉivu ceṉṟu kāmaṅ kāḻppēṟi niṟkum ulakattu makkaḷiṉ kāma uṇarccikkum, civapirāṉ ṟiruvaruḷilēyē niṉivaḻunti avvaruḷ viḷakkamākavē tam kātalari niṉikkuñ cuntarar paraviyāriṉ teyvak kātal uṇarccikkum evvaḷavu vēṟṟumi! tammuḷ otta iyalpiṉarāṉa teyvak kātalar maṇavāḻkkikkuc cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ maṇa vāḻkki yiṉuñ ciṟantatoṉṟaṉi ivvulakil vēṟeṅkaṇuṅ kāṇṭalaritu. iṅṅaṉamē, ivar tam maṟṟi maṉiviyarāṉa caṅkili nācciyārkkum ivarkkum uṇṭāṉa toṭarpun teyvak kātaliṉpāṟ paṭṭatākum.
aṟṟēl, oruvar iruvarik kātalittal iyalumō veṉiṉ; āṇmakaṟ kāyiṉ aḵtiyalum; eṉṉi?
perumiyum uraṉum āṭūumēṉa
eṉṟum,
 accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum muntuṟatta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriyeṉpa
eṉṟum āciriyar tolkāppiyār āṇpeṇ iyaṟki vevvēṟātali aṟivuṟuttalāṉum, ivviyaṟki vēṟupāṭṭiṉi naṉkuṇarntē nākarika makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉuḷ ōrāṇmakaṉ maṉiviyar palari maṇakkap palarum icital pōl oru peṇ makaṉ āṇmakkaḷ palari maṇakka erum icivatillāmiyāṉum, cuntaramūrttikaḷ iruvarik kātalittal āṇpā liyaṟkikku ottatēyām ākalāṉum eṉpatu.
mēluñ, cuntara mūrttikaḷiṉ teyvat taṉmikku otta peṇpālār iruvar iruppa, appeṇpālār iruvariṉ teyvak kātaṟṟaṉmikku otta āṇpālār iruvar ilarāyc cuntaramūrttikaḷ oruvarē uḷarāyiṉ, avar kātalālum aruḷāluma untappaṭṭu iruvari maṇattal vaḻakkēyām. itupōṉṟa ariya kātal nikaḻcciyiṉi nakkīrar māṇikkavācakar mutalāṉa tollāciriyarum uṭaṉpaṭṭut taḻuvutal niṉiviṟ patikkappāṟṟu. ivvāṟu iruvaruyirum uruki oṉṟupaṭṭu niṟkum pēriṉ niliyō ṭoppatākaliṉ, aṉpuruvām iṟivaṉum avartam teyvak kātaṟ peruṅkiḻamikku ottu utaviyāy niṉṟaruḷiṉaṉ. itaṉālaṉṟō cuntaramūrttikaḷ,
paṉmayatta moḻipparavi caṅkilikkum eṉakkum
paṟṟāya perumāṉē maṟṟāri yuṭiyēṉ
eṉṟaruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.

11. cātivēṟṟumi aṉpukku māṟu
kātalaṉpu nikaḻuṅ kālattu atu nikaḻap peṟuvār iruvar cāti kula vēṟṟumi ciṟitum pārātu avvaṉpiṉ vaḻiyarāyc ceṉṟu oruṅku cērvareṉpatum, cāti kula vēṟupāṭukaḷiyē pārttuk koṇṭu iṟumāntirukkum potumakkaḷ tūya kātalaṉpu nikaḻap peṟutaṟkut takkārik kaṇṭukoḷḷa māṭṭuvāralla reṉpatum, aruḷirakkam vāynta iṟivaṉē orōvorukāṟ kātalaṉpuṭiyāri oṉṟu cērttaṟ poruṭṭup potumakkaḷ ākkuñ cātivēṟṟumik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷi muṟṟac citippaṉeṉpatum, cuntaramūrttikaḷ eṉṉuñ civa antaṇar perumāṉ uruttikaṇikiyar kulattiṟ piṟanta paravi nācciyāriyum vēḷāḷar kulattiṟ piṟanta caṅkili nācciyāriyun tamatu kātal ceṉṟa vaḻiyē maṇantu koṇṭamiyāṉum, ataṟkumuṉ avartañ cuṟṟattār avaratu teyvamāṭci aṟiyātē avariyalpukkut takāta oruttiyit tamatu marapiṉiṉṟum eṭuttu maṇam poruttap pukkāliyil ataṉi iṟivaṉē nēr niṉṟu taṭiceytu, piṉṉar avariyalpukku muḻutumotta paravi caṅkiliyāri maṇam poruntiṉamiyāṉun teḷiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa vallavō? kātalaṉpu koḷḷattakkār iruvarip potumakkaḷ aṟiya māṭṭamiyum, iṟivaṉē avari yaṟintu oṉṟu cērttalum māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyta,
 coṟpāl amiḻtivaḷ yāṉ cuvi yeṉṉat tuṇintiṅṅaṉē
naṟpāl niṉitteyvan tantiṉṟu nāṉiva ḷāmpakutip
poṟpuā raṟivār puliyūrp puṉitaṉ potiyilveṟpiṟ
kaṟpā viyavari vāykkaṭitōṭṭa kaḷavakattē
eṉṉum arumit tirukkōviyār ceyyuḷāṉum iṉitu pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa vallavō?
ākavē, civacamayattiṟ piṟanta ovvoruvarun, tam camayāciriya rākiya cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉār niliniṟuttik kāṭṭiya kātaliṉpa vāḻkki ulakil niṉṟu nilavumāṟu tam āṇ peṇ makkaḷi attakiya uṇmiyaṉpiṉ maṉivāḻkkiyil amaracceytu, namatu paḻantamiḻk koḷkiyākiya civa camaya nalattiṉi eṅkum parappi, nam makkaḷip paḻipāvaṅkaḷukku āḷākkātu iḷami toṭṭē avarkaḷatu uḷḷam uṇmiyaṉpil ūṟi oḷirum vaṇṇañ ceytal mikavum iṉṟiyami yātatākum.
iṉip, paḻantamiḻk kōṭpāṭākiya civacamaya uṇmi ivvāṟiruppavum, itaṉi aṟiyamāṭṭārāy, āriyak kurukkaḷmār tamatu nalattiṉ poruṭṭup puṉintu kaṭṭikoṇṭa cāti vēṟṟumiyiṉik kuruṭṭup piṭiyāyp piṭittu, avvavarun tattam cātivarampu kaṭavātu tattam iṉattilēyē uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, apparum appūtikaḷum cātivēṟṟumi pārātu oruṅkiruntu aḷavaḷāvi uṇavu koṇṭatum, cuntaramūrttikaḷum aṅṅaṉamē cātivēṟṟumi pārātu kātalaṉpiṉ vaḻicceṉṟu paravi caṅkiliyāra maṇantu koṇṭatum apperiyārkku okkum. maṟṟu, avarpōl uyarnta pēraṉpu niliyiṉiṉṟu aṉpillā iḻinta nilakkaṇ niṟkum nammaṉōr cātikula vēṟṟumi kaṭantu avvāṟu uṇṇal kalattalkaḷic ceytalākāteṉṟun tammik koḻutta civareṉak kūṟip perumi pārāṭṭik koḷvār cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivartaṅ kūṟṟu nammaṉōri muṉṉēṟṟam aṭiya veṭṭātu paḻipāvaṅkaṭ paṭukkun tīmiyuṭit tātaliyum, paṇṭi nallāciriyar koḷkikaḷellāñ cātivarampip citittoḻikkum peṟṟiya vātaliyuñ cāti vēṟṟumiyum pōlic civarum eṉṉum ematu nūlil virittuk kāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēm. iṅkēyum ivar kūṟṟup piḻi pāṭātalic ciṟitu kāṭṭukiṉṟēm. uyarntōrākiya nam āciriyar kāṭṭiya ōr oḻukkam ciṟanta toṉṟāyiṉ, atu piḻipāṭilatāyiṉ avarvaḻi niṟkum nām avar kāṭṭiya ammuṟiyē oḻukak kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm; avvāṟaṉṟi avarkāṭṭiya avvoḻukkañ ciṟanta tallātāyp piḻipaṭuva toṉṟāyiṉ nām ataṉip piṉpaṟṟi naṭattal ākātu. apparum appūtikaḷuñ cātikula vēṟupāṭukaḷiyē nōkkikkoṇṭiruntaṉarāyiṉ avarkkuḷḷa oppuyarvillāp pēraṉpu paḻutupaṭum; ātalāl, uyarntatākiya aṉpinōkki iḻintatākiya cātivēṟṟumiyiṉi avar appāṟ paṭuttiṉar aṅṅaṉamē, cuntaramūrttikaḷuñ cāti vēṟṟumiyiṉip peritāka niṉintiruntaṉarāyiṉ, tammōṭotta civanēyac ciṟappum pēraṟivum pēraḻakum vāynta paraviyār caṅkiliyār eṉṉum nallāriṇakkatti iḻanta viṭṭavarākal vēṇṭum; maṟṟuppuṉitamākiya avaruḷḷat teḻunta peruṅ kātalaṉpō accāti vēṟṟumiyiṉi nuṟuṅkat tolittup peṟaṟkariya annallāriṇakkattiyē nāṭic ceṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, muṟṟat tuṟanta paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,
nallā riṇakkamum niṉpūci nēcamum ñāṉamumē
allātu vēṟu niliyuḷatō
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉa rallarō? oruvarkku uṭalum, uyirumāy irukkattakkār avarāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭavarē yallāṟ piṟarallarē. ēṉiyōr iṇakkamellāñ ciṟcilakālamē nilippatu. tāytantiya riṇakkam, putalvariṇakkam, āciriyariṇakkam, tōḻariṇakkam, uṭaṉpiṟantāriṇakkam mutaliyaṉa vellāñ ciṟcila kālamēnikaḻvataṉṟi, muḻutun toṭarntu nikaḻā. maṟṟuk, kātalaṉpiṟ ciṟanta kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum iṇaṅkum iṇakkamō avartam vāḻ nāḷelliyaḷavum iravum pakalum piriyātāyt toṭarntu nikaḻun taṉmiyatām. atilum avar aḻakilum aṟivilum aṉpiluñ civanēyattilum mikkavarāyiruntāl avartam iṇakkattiṉ māṭciyiṉiyum iṉpattiṉiyum eṅṅaṉam urikkēm! evvāṟu pukaḻvēm! pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! cuntaramūrttikaṭkum avartam maṉiviyarkkum vāytta nēyamāṭci emmāl aḷapparitākaliṉ avar cātivēṟṟumi tolittuk kikkoṇṭa annēyam emmaṉōrk vaḻikāṭṭum maṅkāp peruviḷakkamāy irukkaṟpāla taṉṟō!

12. cāti vēṟṟumi evarkkum ākātu
periyōrāṉa avarkkāyiṉ cātivēṟṟumi kaṭattaltakum, ciṟiyōrāṉa emakkō atu takātu eṉpirēṟ, ciṟiyōrāṉa yām periyōrātal eppaṭi? periyōr kāṭṭiya naṉ muṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟi yoḻukiṉālaṉṟō yāmum periyōrākalām. avar kāṭṭiya naṉmuṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟātu ennēramuñ cāticcaṇṭiyilēyē cerukkuṟṟu māḻkuvamāyiṉ yām periyōrātal evvāṟu kikūṭum? avar kāṭṭiya pēraṉpu muṟiyiṉikkaṭip piṭiyātu ennēramum aṉpukku māṟāṉa vampilēyē nāṅkālaṅkaḻittāl nām periyōrātal yāṅṅaṉam kikūṭum? periyōr ceytatoṉṟu takātateṉa namakkut tōṉṟu māyiṉ, ataṉi nām ceyyātu viṭalām. apparum appūtiyum pēraṉpāl aḷavaḷāvic cātivēṟṟumiyiṉip pārātu oḻukiyamuṟi uyarntatā, tāḻntatatā? tāḻnta teṉṟu kūṟa evarumē oruppaṭārātalāl, uyarnta annaṉmuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi naṭattalāl namakkuvarum iḻukkeṉṉi? ellā makkaḷum oru tantiyiṉ piḷḷikaḷē yātalāl avarellārum uṭaṉpiṟappurimi pārāṭṭi ellāvaṟṟālum oruṅku aḷavaḷāvutalē naṉṟeṉṟu ēcunātarum makamatu napiyuṅ kūṟiya aṟivurikaḷik kēṭṭu mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷellārum ētoru  vēṟu pāṭumiṉṟi oṉṟupaṭṭu oḻuki ulakiṟ cīruñ ciṟappum eyti varutalik kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭuvittum, nam teyva āciriyarkaḷ oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṟu muṭittukkūṟiya aṟavuriyiṉi nām kaṭippiṭiyātu vāḻnāḷ elliyaḷavuñ cātiyiṟumāppup pēcciyē pēcik, kūṟṟuvaṉukku iriyāy oḻital piṟanāṭṭavarāṟ peritum iḻitturikkap paṭukiṉṟataṉṟō? cāti vēṟṟumiyāl nām peṟṟa nalam eṉṉi? oru ciṟu nalamāyiṉum iruntāl ataṉi nām kikkoḷḷalām. āṉāl, oruvaṉ taṉṉiyuyarnta cātiyāṉākac cerukkuṭaṉ uyarttuppēcip piṟaṉoruvaṉi iḻinta cātiyāṉāka iḻitturikkuṅkāl, aṅṅaṉam iḻikkappaṭṭavaṉ evvaḷavu maṉam nikiṉṟāṉ! avaṉ āṇmi yuṭiyavaṉā yiruntāl taṉṉi iḻittup pēciṉavaṉit tāṉum iḻittup pēca ataṉāl iruvarkkuḷḷuṅ kalām viḷikiṉṟataṉṟō? ivvāṟu aṉpiṉic citittu oṉṟumiyiṉip pāḻpaṭuttuñ cātivēṟṟumi yiṉi nam āciriyaṉmā rellārum oruṅkē  yutittuttaḷḷi aṉpiṉ vaḻiyarāy naṭantukāṭṭiyirukkiyil, nām avarip piṉpaṟṟātu pōvōmāyiṉ, atu nam āciriyarkkuc ceyta perum piḻiyāy nammic civacamayattiṟkup puṟampākkumeṉṟu tiṇṇamāy uṇarmiṉkaḷ!
aṟṟaṉṟu, nam āciriyar naṭantukāṭṭiya muṟikaḷi nām avar nilikku ētum variyiṟ piṉpaṟṟalākāteṉṟurippīr kaḷāyiṉ; nam āciriyaṉmār aṉpukkē uyarvu koṭuttuc cātivēṟṟumiyiṉit tāḻttiṉāl, nām avarkku māṟāyc cātikku uyarvu koṭuttu aṉpik kīḻppaṭuttal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷ poycollātē puṟaṅkūṟātē eṉṟāl, nām poycollip puṟaṅkūṟit tirital vēṇṭum; avarkaḷ kuṭiyātē, koli ceyyātē yeṉṟāl, nāmkuṭittuk koliceytal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷ cilavatti vaṇaṅku, aṭiyārkkut toṇṭucey eṉṟāl, nāmcivatti vaṇaṅkāmal aṭiyārkkut tīmi ceytoḻukal vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāṟ kīḻnilikkaṇ niṟkum nām mēl nilikkaṇ uḷḷār coṉṉavikaḷiyuñ ceytavikaḷiyum ēṟṟal kūṭāteṉpatu kuruṭṭuc civar koḷkiyaṉṟō? āṉāl, ikkuruṭṭuc civar kūṟṟi nammaṉōr kēṭṭu ataṉpaṭi naṭakkap pukuvarāyiṉ innāṭu kāṭāy, innāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa makkaḷ kāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa vilaṅkiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷāy māṟutal tiṇṇamākaliṉ, ēṉi nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷōṭoppa nāmuṅ kalviyiluṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum aṉpilum oṟṟumiyilum vāḻkki yiṉpattilum mēṉ mēl muṉṉēṟṟam aṭitaliyē perunōkkamāyk koṇṭa, nam centamiḻt tollāciriyarākiya tol kāppiyaṉārum avarvaḻi piḻiyātu vanta tiruvaḷḷuvar civa nāyaṉmār civakuravar cantāṉakuravar mutalāṉa cāṉṟōruñ colliyuñ ceytuṅ kāṭṭiya aṉpiṉi uyirāyk koṇṭa iṉpa vāḻkkiyil vāḻtalilēyē karuttūṉṟip kaṭavōmāka!

13. celvap poruḷ iyalpu
iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum
eṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār vakutta mūṉṟil mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iṉpattippaṟṟi itukāṟum ārāyntōm. iṉi, immikkaṇ nikaḻum iṉpavāḻkkiyāṉatu, vaṟumiyum ataṉāl varun tuṉpamum iṉṟi iṉitu naṭipeṟutaṟkum, iṅṅaṉamē maṟumiyiṉpatti vēṭṭuc ceyyap paṭuvatākiya tavavoḻukkam piṟarutaviyi etirpārāmaṟ ceyyappaṭutaṟkuñ celvap poruḷ iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvateṉat tolkāppiyar,
 mēviya ciṟappiṉ ēṉōr paṭimiya
mulli mutalāc colliya muṟiyāṟ
piḻittatu piḻiyā tākal vēṇṭiyum
iḻitta oṇporuḷ muṭiyavum pirivē
eṉṟu kūṟutalāṉum, tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār,
 aruḷillārk kavvulakam illi poruḷillārk
kivvulakam illāki yāṅku
eṉṟum,
aṟaṉīṉum iṉpamum īṉun tiṟaṉaṟintu
tītiṉṟi vanta poruḷ
eṉṟuṅ kūṟutalāṉuñ celvap poruḷ īṭṭum vakiyil nam āciriyar koṇṭa karuttum nammāl ārāyaṟ pālatāyirukkiṉṟatu. celvapporuḷ iṅṅaṉam immi maṟumip pēṟukaḷi aṭivittaṟku iṉṟiyamiyākkaruviyā yiruttalāṉuṅ, kalviyaṟivu vāyātavar kaṭku ataṉi oḻuṅkāṉa naṉmuṟiyil īṭṭumvakiyum, īṭṭiya apporuḷi avvāṟē naṉmuṟiyiṟ celavu ceytu immi maṟumi yiṉpaṅkaḷi eytum vakiyuñ ciṟitumē viḷaṅkā. atuvēyumaṉṟi, orukāl ūḻviṉi vayattāṟ kalvi yaṟivu vāyātavarkaṭkuc celvam variṉ, ataṉip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻi teriyārāy, ataṉiyē teyvamākakkoṇṭu avar immi maṟumi nalaṅkaḷi oruṅkē iḻantuviṭṭuc cilanāḷil apporuḷi iṅkēyē vittuviṭṭu iṟantoḻitaliyum, avarkkup piṉ apporuḷik kikkoḷvōr kuṭikkuṅ kūttikkuṅ kolikkuṅ kōṇavaḻakkukkum veṟum paṭṭap peyarkkum vāriyiṟittu ataṉic cilanāṭkaḷil aḻittuviṭutaliyuṅ kaṇṇetirē kāṇkiṉṟēm. ākavē, niliyillāta celvap poruḷi naṉmuṟiyil īṭṭutalum, ataṉi naṉmuṟiyiṟ payaṉpaṭuttalum niliyāṉa ciṟanta kalvipporuḷic ciṟantamuṟiyiṟ peṟṟārkkaṉṟi ēṉiyōrkkuk kikūṭāmi eḷitil aṟiyappaṭum.

14. kalvipporuḷ māṭci
ivvuṇmiyi naṉṟākap pākupaṭuttuṇarnta nam muṉṉōrkaḷ poruḷippaṟṟip pēca varuṅkā lellām, ataṉik kalvipporuḷ celvapporuḷ eṉa iru kūṟākap pakuttu, mutaliṟ kalvipporuḷ māṭciyiṉi valiyuṟuttip pēci, ataṉpiṟakē celvap poruḷip paṟṟip pēcāniṟ
kiṉṟaṉar. itu, nam mutalāciriyarāṉa tolkāppiyaṉār ivviruvakip poruḷiyum peṟum poruṭṭu iḷiñarkaḷ vēṟu nāṭu nōkkic cellum pirivukaḷil nūl ōtutaṟkup piriyum pirivi muṉvittup, poruḷtēṭutaṟkup piriyum pirivi ataṉpiṉ vittu akattiṇiyiya liṟ pirivilakkaṇam vakutturittamiyāṉum, avarvaḻi vanta tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉārum aṅṅaṉamē poruṭpāliṟ kalviyi muṉvittu; ataṟkup pala atikāraṅkaḷ kaṭantupōyc celvattip piṉvit turittamiyāṉum naṉkaṟikiṉṟēm. eṉavē, oruvarkku eññāṉṟum aḻiyāp peruñ celvamāvatun, taṉṉip payiṉṟāri eḻupiṟappun toṭarntu celvatun, taṉṉit toṭarntu payilvārkku ammuṟiyē avaraṟiviṉi mēlum mēlum viḷaṅkac ceyvatun, taṉṉip payiṉṟavariyē kaṇṇuṭiyavarākkit taṉṉip payilātavar kaṇṇip puṇṇeṉac ceyvatuṅ kalvic celvamē eṉpār,
 kēṭil viḻuccelvaṅ kalvi yoruvaṟku
māṭalla maṟṟi yavi
 eṉṟum,
 orumikkaṭ ṭāṉkaṟṟa kalvi yoruvaṟ
keḻumiyum ēmāp puṭittu
 eṉavum,
 toṭṭaṉit tūṟum maṇaṟkēṇi māntarkkuk
kaṟṟaṉit tūṟum aṟivu
eṉavum,
kaṇṇuṭiya reṉpavar kaṟṟōr mukattiraṇṭu
puṇṇuṭiyar kallā tavar
eṉavun teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvarum ōtiyaruḷiṉār. āriya nūlār poruḷaṟṟa karmaṅkaṭkum piṟappiṉāṟ cātikkum uyarvu collutal pōl, nam āciriyar evaruñ collāmiyum, nam āciriyar ellāruṅ kalvikkē uyarvu collik kalvi kallāmaṟ cātipaṟṟi uyarvu tēṭuvārkku iḻivu collutalum, āriya nūlār oru cila vakuppiṉarē kalvi kaṟṟaṟkuriyar, ēṉiyōr ataṟkuriyarallar eṉṟu collutal pōlātu, nam āciriyaṉmārellāruṅ kalvi kallātavar evarāyiruppiṉum avar vilaṅkukaḷi yoppareṉa valiyuṟuttalum:
 mēṟpiṟantā rāyiṉuṅ kallātār kīḻppiṟantuṅ
kaṟṟār aṉittilar pāṭu	(tirukkuṟaḷ)
eṉṟum,
 kallāta mūṭarik kāṇavum ākātu
